# 项目: contamcal-irt-calibration

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: cqa1-21977
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.cqa1.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/contamcal-irt-calibration

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 提交实验前必须检查目标 GPU 上是否已有同类进程（nvidia-smi 查 PID + 对比 output dir），有则不提交（原因：GPU 2 因重复提交导致 4 次进程冲突和数据污染。on_failed hook 触发的 worker 和手动提交都不做查重。）
- **C002**: 提交 GPU 任务前必须用 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 空闲，不得仅依赖 get_project_resource_usage 判断（原因：resource_usage 工具因 PID/tmux 不匹配严重误报（cuda:0/2/3 活跃但报 idle），已导致调度风险。Reviewer 2026-05-14 确认。）
- **C003**: 标记实验为 failed 时必须同时 kill 所有关联 GPU 进程；submit 新任务到 GPU 前必须先 nvidia-smi + ps 确认该 GPU 无残留进程（原因：GPU 2 两次出现"标 failed 不 kill 进程"导致新旧进程冲突、数据腐败风险。v3 标 failed 但 2 个子进程存活，v4 提交时未检查，造成三进程竞争同一 output-dir。）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格

&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：在 GPU 1 上提交 0.5B d100 seed=123 SFT

### 背景
D025 multi-seed 策略，扩大 N_models。0.5B seed=123 系列。

### 超参数（与 0.5B seed=42 实验保持一致）
- **lr=2e-4, lora_alpha=32, lora_r=16, seed=123**
- 注意：D020 的 lr=5e-5/alpha=16 是 3B 专用，0.5B 必须沿用 lr=2e-4/alpha=32

### 服务器
- cqa1-21977 (SSH config name)
- GPU: **仅用 cuda:1**
- 项目路径: /root/contamcal-irt-calibration
- 环境: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base

### 步骤

1. **确认 GPU 1 空闲**（ssh_read_only）:
   ```
   nvidia-smi -i 1 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name --format=csv,noheader
   ```
   如果有进程在跑，报告并停止。

2. **确认数据和模型存在**（ssh_read_only）:
   ```
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/data/sft_dosage_100.jsonl
   ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-0.5B
   ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-0.5B_d100_s123 2>/dev/null && echo "EXISTS" || echo "NOT_EXISTS"
   ```

3. **读取 train_sft.py 的 --gpu 参数行为**（ssh_read_only）:
   ```
   head -50 /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/train_sft.py
   grep -n "gpu" /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/train_sft.py
   ```
   确认 --gpu 是物理卡号还是相对编号。**已知 feedback：--gpu 必须等于物理卡号，不是 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 相对编号。**

4. **用 submit_training_job 提交**:
   - exp_id: exp_001_sft_05B_d100_s123
   - server: cqa1-21977
   - gpu: cuda:1
   - command（根据 --gpu 行为调整）:
   ```
   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && /root/miniconda3/bin/python /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/train_sft.py --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-0.5B --data-path /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/data/sft_dosage_100.jsonl --output-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-0.5B_d100_s123 --lr 2e-4 --lora-alpha 32 --lora-r 16 --seed 123 --gpu 1
   ```
   - hypothesis: "0.5B d100 seed=123 SFT for D025 multi-seed strategy (lr=2e-4, alpha=32, matching 0.5B s42 params)"

5. **提交后验证**（等 30 秒后 ssh_read_only）:
   ```
   nvidia-smi -i 1 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv,noheader
   ```

### 注意
- 只用 GPU 1，不碰其他卡
- --gpu 参数必须等于物理卡号（feedback 确认）
- 必须用 submit_training_job 提交，不要 nohup

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

LoRA config\\n    lora_config = LoraConfig(\\n        r=args.lora_r,\\n        lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha,\\n        lora_dropout=DEFAULT_LORA_CONFIG[\\\"lora_dropout\\\"],\\n        target_modules=DEFAULT_LORA_CONFIG[\\\"target_modules\\\"],\\n        task_type=TaskType.CAUSAL_LM,\\n        bias=\\\"none\\\",\\n    )\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"LoRA config: r=%d, alpha=%d, targets=%s\\\",\\n                lora_config.r, lora_config.lora_alpha, lora_config.target_modules)\\n\\n    # Load dataset\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading training data: %s\\\", args.data_path)\\n    dataset = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=args.data_path, split=\\\"train\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training samples: %d\\\", len(dataset))\\n\\n    # Training arguments\\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\\n        output_dir=str(output_dir / \\\"lora_adapter\\\"),\\n        num_train_epochs=args.epochs,\\n        per_device_train_batch_size=args.batch_size,\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.grad_accum,\\n        learning_rate=args.lr,\\n        warmup_ratio=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"warmup_ratio\\\"],\\n        bf16=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"bf16\\\"],\\n        logging_steps=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"logging_steps\\\"],\\n        save_strategy=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"save_strategy\\\"],\\n        save_total_limit=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"save_total_limit\\\"],\\n        report_to=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"report_to\\\"],\\n        optim=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"optim\\\"],\\n        lr_scheduler_type=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"lr_scheduler_type\\\"],\\n        max_grad_norm=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"max_grad_norm\\\"],\\n        dataloader_num_workers=DEFAULT_TRAINING_ARGS[\\\"dataloader_num_workers\\\"],\\n        seed=args.seed,\\n        max_length=args.max_seq_length,\\n        dataset_text_field=None,\\n    )\\n\\n    # Trainer\\n    trainer = SFTTrainer(\\n        model=model,\\n        args=training_args,\\n        train_dataset=dataset,\\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\\n        peft_config=lora_config,\\n        formatting_func=formatting_func,\\n    )\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Starting training...\\\")\\n    trainer.train()\\n\\n    # Save LoRA adapter\\n    adapter_dir = output_dir / \\\"lora_adapter\\\"\\n    trainer.save_model(str(adapter_dir))\\n    tokenizer.save_pretrained(str(adapter_dir))\\n    logger.info(\\\"LoRA adapter saved to %s\\\", adapter_dir)\\n\\n    # Optionally merge and save full model (off by default to save disk)\\n    if args.merge:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Merging LoRA adapter into base model...\\\")\\n        merged_model = trainer.model.merge_and_unload()\\n        merged_dir = output_dir / \\\"merged\\\"\\n        merged_model.save_pretrained(str(merged_dir))\\n        tokenizer.save_pretrained(str(merged_dir))\\n        logger.info(\\\"Merged model saved to %s\\\", merged_dir)\\n\\n    # Save training config\\n    config = {\\n        \\\"model_name\\\": args.model_name,\\n        \\\"data_path\\\": args.data_path,\\n        \\\"lora_r\\\": args.lora_r,\\n        \\\"lora_alpha\\\": args.lora_alpha,\\n        \\\"epochs\\\": args.epochs,\\n        \\\"batch_size\\\": args.batch_size,\\n        \\\"grad_accum\\\": args.grad_accum,\\n        \\\"lr\\\": args.lr,\\n        \\\"max_seq_length\\\": args.max_seq_length,\\n        \\\"seed\\\": args.seed,\\n        \\\"num_samples\\\": len(dataset),\\n    }\\n    with open(output_dir / \\\"train_config.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(config, f, indent=2)\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}

dosage-0 models: their own raw accuracy\\n    # dosage>0 models: d=0 mean as counterfactual reference\\n    ground_truth = np.full(len(dosages), gt_acc_d0)\\n    ground_truth[d0_mask] = raw_acc[d0_mask]\\n\\n    return ground_truth\\n\\n\\ndef load_exp002_signals(signals_dir, models, benchmark, signal_key=\\\"s1_embedding\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load exp-002 merged signals -> per-model contamination score.\\n    D023: replaces oracle dosage with detected contamination signals.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    scores = []\\n    for m in models:\\n        # quick test\\n    python run_exp006.py                                         # full run\\n\\nDependencies: cmdstanpy, numpy, scipy\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))\\nfrom run_irt_calibration import (\\n    load_eval_results,\\n    load_pooled_eval_results,\\n    load_exposure_scores,\\n    build_exposure_matrix,\\n    run_benchmark_fit,\\n    STAN_MODELS,\\n)\\nfrom baselines import run_all_baselines\\nfrom evaluate_calibration import (\\n    evaluate_method,\\n    format_text_table,\\n    format_latex_table,\\n)\\nfrom joint_fit import run_joint_fit\\n\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n# D018: 3B GSM8K excluded from dose-response analysis\\nD018_EXCLUDE = [(\\\"3B\\\", \\\"gsm8k\\\")]\\n\\n\\ndef should_exclude_dose_response(model_name, benchmark, rules=D018_EXCLUDE):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check D018 exclusion rules for dose-response analysis.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for pattern, bench in rules:\\n        if pattern in model_name and benchmark == bench:\\n            return True\\n    return False\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ground_truth(responses, dosages):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Ground truth = accuracy of dosage-0 models (uncontaminated).\\n\\n    For dosage-0 models, raw accuracy IS the clean accuracy.\\n    For dosage>0 models, we use the mean d=0 accuracy as the\\n    counterfactual reference (what they would score without contamination).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    raw_acc = responses.mean(axis=1)\\n    d0_mask = dosages == 0\\n\\n    if d0_mask.sum() > 0:\\n        gt_acc_d0 = float(raw_acc[d0_mask].mean())\\n    else:\\n        gt_acc_d0 = float(raw_acc.mean())\\n\\n    # dosage-0 models: their own raw accuracy\\n    # dosage>0 models: d=0 mean as counterfactual reference\\n    ground_truth = np.full(len(dosages), gt_acc_d0)\\n    ground_truth[d0_mask] = raw_acc[d0_mask]\\n\\n    return ground_truth\\n\\n\\ndef load_exp002_signals(signals_dir, models, benchmark, signal_key=\\\"s1_embedding\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load exp-002 merged signals -> per-model contamination score.\\n    D023: replaces oracle dosage with detected contamination signals.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    scores = []\\n    for m in models:\\n        #
